AThe Asubobjects Ahold Athat Asection’s Asettings. AUse Aa Acarriage Areturn Acharacter Afollowed Aby Aa Anewline A("\r\n") Ainstead Aof Aa Anewline Acharacter Ato Adifferentiate Alines. AThis Awill Amake Athe Asplit Amethod Aenable Aa Aregular Aexpression Aas Aits Aargument, Ayou Acan Autilize Aa Aregular Aexpression Alike A/\r?\n/ Ato Adivide Ait Ain Aa Away Athat Aenables Aboth A"\n" Aand A"\r\n" Abetween Alines.

function AparseINI(string) A{

let Aresult A= A{};

let Asection A= Aresult;

section[match[1]] A= Amatch[2];
